TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HiPAN的分层姿态自适应导航框架，通过高层策略生成导航命令、低层控制器执行运动，结合路径引导的课程学习，使四足机器人在非结构化3D环境中实现高效导航，仿真和实物实验均优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统顺序建图-规划方法在非结构化3D环境中存在累积感知误差大、计算开销高的问题，难以部署在资源受限的四足机器人平台上，亟需一种直接在机载深度图像上运行的高效导航方案。

Method: HiPAN采用分层设计：高层策略生成平面速度与身体姿态等导航命令，低层控制器执行姿态自适应运动；同时引入路径引导的课程学习，从反应式避障逐步扩展到长视野策略导航。

Result: 仿真中HiPAN在导航成功率和路径效率上均优于经典反应式规划器和端到端基线方法，真实实验验证了其在多种非结构化3D环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: HiPAN通过分层导航与课程学习有效解决了四足机器人在复杂3D环境中的长视野导航难题，兼顾了姿态适应与计算效率，为资源受限平台上的自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Navigating quadruped robots in unstructured 3D environments poses significant challenges, requiring goal-directed motion, effective exploration to escape from local minima, and posture adaptation to traverse narrow, height-constrained spaces. Conventional approaches employ a sequential mapping-planning pipeline but suffer from accumulated perception errors and high computational overhead, restricting their applicability on resource-constrained platforms. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Posture-Adaptive Navigation (HiPAN), a framework that operates directly on onboard depth images at deployment. HiPAN adopts a hierarchical design: a high-level policy generates strategic navigation commands (planar velocity and body posture), which are executed by a low-level, posture-adaptive locomotion controller. To mitigate myopic behaviors and facilitate long-horizon navigation, we introduce Path-Guided Curriculum Learning, which progressively extends the navigation horizon from reactive obstacle avoidance to strategic navigation. In simulation, HiPAN achieves higher navigation success rates and greater path efficiency than classical reactive planners and end-to-end baselines, while real-world experiments further validate its applicability across diverse, unstructured 3D environments.

</details>


### [113] [3D Generation for Embodied AI and Robotic Simulation: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26509)
*Tianwei Ye,Yifan Mao,Minwen Liao,Jian Liu,Chunchao Guo,Dazhao Du,Quanxin Shou,Fangqi Zhu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了面向具身AI的3D生成技术，从数据生成器、仿真环境和Sim2Real桥梁三个角色组织文献，指出该领域正从视觉真实感向交互就绪性转变，并分析了物理标注不足、几何质量与物理有效性脱节、评估碎片化、仿真到现实差距等关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和机器人系统对3D内容的需求已超越视觉真实感，需要具备运动学结构、物理材质、可交互场景等特性。然而现有3D生成研究分散，缺乏从具身智能角度统一梳理的综述，亟需系统性分析以推动该领域发展。

Method: 从三个角色组织文献：(1) 数据生成器：生成可交互的刚体/铰接体/可变形物体；(2) 仿真环境：构建结构化、可控、智能体的任务导向场景；(3) Sim2Real桥梁：支持数字孪生重建、数据增强和合成演示。

Result: 揭示了该领域从视觉真实感向交互就绪性的范式转变；识别出四大瓶颈：物理标注数据有限、几何质量与物理有效性之间的鸿沟、评估标准碎片化、仿真到现实迁移的持续挑战。

Conclusion: 3D生成要成为具身智能的可靠基础，必须解决物理有效性、标准化评估和Sim2Real鸿沟等核心问题，未来应更关注交互就绪性而非单纯的视觉真实感。

Abstract: Embodied AI and robotic systems increasingly depend on scalable, diverse, and physically grounded 3D content for simulation-based training and real-world deployment. While 3D generative modeling has advanced rapidly, embodied applications impose requirements far beyond visual realism: generated objects must carry kinematic structure and material properties, scenes must support interaction and task execution, and the resulting content must bridge the gap between simulation and reality. This survey presents the first survey of 3D generation for embodied AI and organizes the literature around three roles that 3D generation plays in embodied systems. In \emph{Data Generator}, 3D generation produces simulation-ready objects and assets, including articulated, physically grounded, and deformable content for downstream interaction; in \emph{Simulation Environments}, it constructs interactive and task-oriented worlds, spanning structure-aware, controllable, and agentic scene generation; and in \emph{Sim2Real Bridge}, it supports digital twin reconstruction, data augmentation, and synthetic demonstrations for downstream robot learning and real-world transfer. We also show that the field is shifting from visual realism toward interaction readiness, and we identify the main bottlenecks, including limited physical annotations, the gap between geometric quality and physical validity, fragmented evaluation, and the persistent sim-to-real divide, that must be addressed for 3D generation to become a dependable foundation for embodied intelligence. Our project page is at https://3dgen4robot.github.io.

</details>


### [114] [LLM-Flax : Generalizable Robotic Task Planning via Neuro-Symbolic Approaches with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26569)
*Seongmin Kim,Daegyu Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LLM-Flax是一个三阶段框架，利用本地LLM和仅PDDL领域文件，完全消除了神经符号任务规划器中需要人工编写规则和监督训练GNN的工作，在MazeNamo基准上实现了平均SR 0.945（超越人工基线的0.828）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号任务规划器部署到新领域需要大量人工工作：领域专家必须编写松弛规则和补充规则，还需要解决数百个训练问题来监督GNN对象评分器。这种人工密集型过程阻碍了规划器的快速部署和扩展。

Method: 提出LLM-Flax三阶段框架：(1) 通过带格式验证和自纠正的结构化提示，自动生成松弛规则和补充规则；(2) LLM引导的失败恢复，采用可行性门控预算策略，在每次LLM调用前预留API延迟成本，防止下游松弛回退被饿死；(3) 用零样本LLM对象重要性评分替代领域训练的GNN，无需训练数据。

Result: 在MazeNamo基准（8个基准，覆盖10x10至15x15网格）上，LLM-Flax平均SR 0.945 vs 人工基线0.828（+0.117）。12x12 Expert上SR 0.733（人工为0.000），15x15 Hard上SR 1.000（人工0.900）。Stage 3在12x12 Hard上SR 0.720，但面临上下文窗口瓶颈。

Conclusion: LLM-Flax成功证明可以用本地LLM完全替代人工规则编写和GNN训练，大幅降低部署成本并提升性能。但零样本对象评分面临上下文窗口扩展性问题，是未来工作的主要挑战。

Abstract: Deploying a neuro-symbolic task planner on a new domain today requires significant manual effort: a domain expert must author relaxation and complementary rules, and hundreds of training problems must be solved to supervise a Graph Neural Network (GNN) object scorer. We propose LLM-Flax, a three-stage framework that eliminates all three sources of manual effort using a locally hosted LLM given only a PDDL domain file. Stage 1 automatically generates relaxation and complementary rules via structured prompting with format validation and self-correction. Stage 2 introduces LLM-guided failure recovery with a feasibility-gated budget policy that explicitly reserves API latency cost before each LLM call, preventing the downstream relaxation fallback from being starved. Stage 3 replaces the domain-trained GNN entirely with zero-shot LLM object importance scoring, requiring no training data. We evaluate all three stages on the MazeNamo benchmark across 10x10, 12x12, and 15x15 grids (8 benchmarks total). LLM-Flax achieves average SR 0.945 versus the manual baseline's 0.828 (+0.117), matching or outperforming manual rules on every one of the eight benchmarks. On 12x12 Expert, LLM-Flax attains SR 0.733 where the manual planner fails entirely (SR 0.000); on 15x15 Hard, it achieves SR 1.000 versus Manual's 0.900. Stage 3 demonstrates feasibility (SR 0.720 on 12x12 Hard with no training data) but faces a context-window bottleneck at scale, pointing to the primary open challenge for future work.

</details>


### [115] [STAR-Filter: Efficient Convex Free-Space Approximation via Starshaped Set Filtering in Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26626)
*Yuwei Wu,Yichen Zhao,Dexter Ong,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STAR-Filter轻量级框架，通过星形集构造作为快速滤波器，在碰撞-free空间中高效生成凸区域（多面体），显著降低计算开销并提升对噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代优化的凸区域膨胀方法在障碍物复杂或传感器噪声大时效率下降，且对初始化和精确几何模型敏感，需要更轻量、鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出STAR-Filter框架，利用星形集（starshaped set）构造作为快速滤波器，识别障碍物点作为主动支撑约束，减少冗余计算，结合凸优化生成多面体。

Result: 在仿真中达到最低计算时间，降低多面体生成的保守性，在真实噪声和大规模数据上表现优异。

Conclusion: STAR-Filter在安全飞行走廊生成和敏捷四旋翼规划中有效，兼具高效率、鲁棒性和低保守性。

Abstract: Approximating collision-free space is fundamental to robot planning in complex environments. Convex geometric representations, such as polytopes and ellipsoids, are widely employed due to their structural properties, which can be easily integrated with convex optimization. Iterative optimization-based inflation methods can generate large volume polytopes in cluttered environments, but their efficiency degrades as the obstacle set becomes more complex or when sensor data are noisy. These methods are also sensitive to initialization and often rely on accurate geometric models. In this paper, we propose the STAR-Filter, a lightweight framework that employs starshaped set construction as a fast filter for convex region generation in collision-free space. By identifying obstacle points as active supporting constraints, the proposed method significantly reduces redundant computation while preserving feasibility and robustness to sensor noise. We provide theoretical and numerical analyses that characterize the structural properties of the starshaped set and proposed pipeline in environments of varying complexity. Simulation results show that the proposed framework achieves the lowest computation time and reduces conservativeness in polytope generation for real-world noisy and large-scale data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework for Safe Flight Corridor (SFC) generation and agile quadrotor planning in noisy environments.

</details>


### [116] [ATLAS: An Annotation Tool for Long-horizon Robotic Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26637)
*Sergej Stanovcic,Daniel Sliwowski,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个专为机器人长时序动作分割设计的标注工具，支持多模态数据同步可视化（多视角视频+本体感受信号），兼容ROS bag和RLDS等主流数据格式，相较于ELAN将标注时间减少至少6%，时间对齐精度提升2.8%，边界误差降低五倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标注工具主要面向纯视觉数据，缺乏对机器人特有信号（如夹爪状态、力/力矩）的原生支持，且适配不同数据集格式需要大量额外工作，难以高效完成长时序机器人演示的动作边界精确标注。

Method: ATLAS通过时间同步可视化多模态机器人数据（多视角视频+本体感受信号），支持动作边界、动作标签和任务结果的标注；原生支持ROS bag和RLDS格式，通过模块化数据集抽象层可轻松扩展至新格式；采用键盘为中心的交互界面提升效率。

Result: 在接触式装配任务实验中，ATLAS相比ELAN将平均每个动作的标注时间减少至少6%；加入时序数据后，与专家标注的时间对齐精度提升超过2.8%，边界误差降低至五分之一（相比纯视觉标注工具）。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过多模态数据同步可视化和高效交互设计，显著提升了机器人长时序动作分割的标注效率和精度，验证了机器人专用标注工具的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: Annotating long-horizon robotic demonstrations with precise temporal action boundaries is crucial for training and evaluating action segmentation and manipulation policy learning methods. Existing annotation tools, however, are often limited: they are designed primarily for vision-only data, do not natively support synchronized visualization of robot-specific time-series signals (e.g., gripper state or force/torque), or require substantial effort to adapt to different dataset formats. In this paper, we introduce ATLAS, an annotation tool tailored for long-horizon robotic action segmentation. ATLAS provides time-synchronized visualization of multi-modal robotic data, including multi-view video and proprioceptive signals, and supports annotation of action boundaries, action labels, and task outcomes. The tool natively handles widely used robotics dataset formats such as ROS bags and the Reinforcement Learning Dataset (RLDS) format, and provides direct support for specific datasets such as REASSEMBLE. ATLAS can be easily extended to new formats via a modular dataset abstraction layer. Its keyboard-centric interface minimizes annotation effort and improves efficiency. In experiments on a contact-rich assembly task, ATLAS reduced the average per-action annotation time by at least 6% compared to ELAN, while the inclusion of time-series data improved temporal alignment with expert annotations by more than 2.8% and decreased boundary error fivefold compared to vision-only annotation tools.

</details>


### [117] [Atomic-Probe Governance for Skill Updates in Compositional Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26689)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,John See,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出配对采样跨版本互换协议来研究机器人技能库更新对组合策略的影响，发现主导技能效应（一个ECM成功率达86.7%，其他≤26.7%），并提出原子质量探针和混合选择器用于技能更新治理，在T6任务上原子探针仅比完全重验证低23pp（64.6% vs 87.5%），混合选择器(m=10)将差距缩小至~12pp且成本降低54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合技能方法（如BLADE、SymSkill等）将技能库视为冻结的，未分析技能替换后组合结果如何变化，缺乏对技能库动态更新的系统性理解。

Method: 提出配对采样跨版本互换协议，在robosuite操作任务上进行实验；发现主导技能效应；提出原子质量探针和混合选择器（结合零成本的原子探针与选择性组合重验证），在144个技能更新决策上刻画Pareto前沿。

Result: 双轴插销任务中发现主导技能效应（86.7% vs ≤26.7%）；T6任务中原子探针匹配率64.6%（完全重验证87.5%），混合选择器(m=10)达~75%，成本仅为完全重验证的46%；跨任务平均原子探针与完全重验证差距在3pp内。

Conclusion: 原子质量探针是首个原理化的、可直接部署的技能更新治理原语，为组合机器人策略中的技能库动态管理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Skill libraries in deployed robotic systems are continually updated through fine-tuning, fresh demonstrations, or domain adaptation, yet existing typed-composition methods (BLADE, SymSkill, Generative Skill Chaining) treat the library as frozen at test time and do not analyze how composition outcomes change when a skill is replaced. We introduce a paired-sampling cross-version swap protocol on robosuite manipulation tasks to characterize this dimension of compositional skill learning. On a dual-arm peg-in-hole task we discover a dominant-skill effect: one ECM achieves 86.7% atomic success rate while every other ECM is at or below 26.7%, and whether this dominant ECM enters a composition shifts the success rate by up to +50pp. We characterize the boundary on a simpler pick task where all atomic policies saturate at 100% and the effect is undefined. Across three tasks we further find that off-policy behavioral distance metrics fail to identify the dominant ECM, ruling out the natural cheap predictor. We propose an atomic-quality probe and a Hybrid Selector combining per-skill probes (zero per-decision cost) with selective composition revalidation (full cost), and characterize its Pareto frontier on 144 skill-update decisions. On T6 the atomic-only probe sits 23pp below full revalidation (64.6% vs 87.5% oracle match) at zero per-decision cost; a Hybrid Selector with m=10 closes most of that gap to ~12pp at 46% of full-revalidation cost. On the cross-task average over 144 events, atomic-only is within 3pp of full revalidation under a mixed-oracle caveat. The atomic-quality probe is, to our knowledge, the first principled, deployment-ready primitive for skill-update governance in compositional robot policies.

</details>


### [118] [Unified 4D World Action Modeling from Video Priors with Asynchronous Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26694)
*Jun Guo,Qiwei Li,Peiyan Li,Zilong Chen,Nan Sun,Yifei Su,Heyun Wang,Yuan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-WAM是一个统一4D世界模型，同时支持实时机器人动作执行和高保真4D世界合成。它利用预训练视频扩散模型，通过多视角RGB-D视频预测未来世界，并提出异步噪声采样（ANS）来平衡生成质量与动作解码效率。在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一世界模型（如UWM）仅建模2D像素空间，无法平衡动作执行效率与世界建模质量。需要一种既能支持实时机器人控制又能生成高质量4D场景的统一框架。

Method: 1）将预训练DiT的最后几层复制为专用深度预测分支，轻量级结构适配获取空间信息；2）提出异步噪声采样（ANS），推理时动作解码用少步数，视频生成用完整步数，训练时从联合分布采样对齐推理分布。

Result: 在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0基准上分别达到79.2%和90.7%的平均成功率，4D重建与生成在视觉和几何指标上均超越现有方法。

Conclusion: X-WAM证明了统一4D世界模型在实时机器人控制与高质量世界建模上可以兼得，为具身智能提供了一种高效的统一框架。

Abstract: We propose X-WAM, a Unified 4D World Model that unifies real-time robotic action execution and high-fidelity 4D world synthesis (video + 3D reconstruction) in a single framework, addressing the critical limitations of prior unified world models (e.g., UWM) that only model 2D pixel-space and fail to balance action efficiency and world modeling quality. To leverage the strong visual priors of pretrained video diffusion models, X-WAM imagines the future world by predicting multi-view RGB-D videos, and obtains spatial information efficiently through a lightweight structural adaptation: replicating the final few blocks of the pretrained Diffusion Transformer into a dedicated depth prediction branch for the reconstruction of future spatial information. Moreover, we propose Asynchronous Noise Sampling (ANS) to jointly optimize generation quality and action decoding efficiency. ANS applies a specialized asynchronous denoising schedule during inference, which rapidly decodes actions with fewer steps to enable efficient real-time execution, while dedicating the full sequence of steps to generate high-fidelity video. Rather than entirely decoupling the timesteps during training, ANS samples from their joint distribution to align with the inference distribution. Pretrained on over 5,800 hours of robotic data, X-WAM achieves 79.2% and 90.7% average success rate on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks, while producing high-fidelity 4D reconstruction and generation surpassing existing methods in both visual and geometric metrics.

</details>


### [119] [Rule-based High-Level Coaching for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Search-and-Rescue UAV Missions Under Limited-Simulation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26833)
*Mahya Ramezani,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合固定规则高层顾问与在线学习低层强化学习控制器的分层决策框架，用于无人机搜索救援任务，在无预训练条件下提升了早期安全性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜索救援场景中，无人机面临模拟训练有限、需要快速适应新环境的挑战，现有方法难以同时保证安全性和适应性。

Method: 提出分层框架：高层基于离线任务规范编译为确定性规则，提供可解释的安全引导（推荐/禁止动作及仲裁权重）；低层通过在线强化学习学习，采用模式感知的优先回放机制并融入规则元数据。

Result: 在电池感知多目标递送和移动目标递送两项任务中，该方法主要通过减少碰撞终止来提升早期安全性和样本效率，同时保留在线适应能力。

Conclusion: 规则与学习结合的分层框架在无预训练的严格条件下能有效平衡安全性与在线适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical decision-making framework for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) missions motivated by search-and-rescue (SAR) scenarios under limited simulation training. The framework combines a fixed rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. To stress-test early adaptation, we also consider a strict no-pretraining deployment regime. The high-level advisor is defined offline from a structured task specification and compiled into deterministic rules. It provides interpretable mission- and safety-aware guidance through recommended actions, avoided actions, and regime-dependent arbitration weights. The low-level controller learns online from task-defined dense rewards and reuses experience through a mode-aware prioritized replay mechanism augmented with rule-derived metadata. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in obstacle-rich environments. Across both tasks, the proposed method improves early safety and sample efficiency primarily by reducing collision terminations, while preserving the ability to adapt online to scenario-specific dynamics.

</details>


### [120] [Walk With Me: Long-Horizon Social Navigation for Human-Centric Outdoor Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26839)
*Lingfeng Zhang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Xizhou Bu,Yingbo Tang,Hongsheng Li,Jinghui Lu,Xiu-shen Wei,Jiayi Ma,Yu Liu,Jing Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Xiaojun Liang,Long Chen,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Walk with Me框架，通过结合GPS、候选兴趣点、高层视觉语言模型和低层视觉语言-动作策略，实现无需预建地图的户外长时序社交导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图方法依赖昂贵预建高精地图，学习策略局限于室内和短时序场景，急需无地图的户外长时序社交导航方案。

Method: 1) 利用GPS和公共地图API的候选兴趣点进行语义目的地定位和路径点提议；2) 高层VLM将抽象指令转化为具体目的地并规划粗略路径序列；3) 观测感知路由机制决定低层VLA策略或高层VLM安全推理的执行时机；4) 复杂场景触发高层推理和停-等行为。

Result: 实现了户外环境下从高级人类指令到安全、长时序、社交合规导航行为的完整映射，在拥挤路口等复杂场景具备安全推理能力。

Conclusion: Walk with Me通过语义意图理解、无图长时序规划、安全感知推理和低层动作生成的结合，为人类辅助场景提供了实用的户外社交导航方案。

Abstract: Assisting humans in open-world outdoor environments requires robots to translate high-level natural-language intentions into safe, long-horizon, and socially compliant navigation behavior. Existing map-based methods rely on costly pre-built HD maps, while learning-based policies are mostly limited to indoor and short-horizon settings. To bridge this gap, we propose Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation from high-level human instructions. Walk with Me leverages GPS context and lightweight candidate points-of-interest from a public map API for semantic destination grounding and waypoint proposal. A High-Level Vision-Language Model grounds abstract instructions into concrete destinations and plans coarse waypoint sequences. During execution, an observation-aware routing mechanism determines whether the Low-Level Vision-Language-Action policy can handle the current situation or whether explicit safety reasoning from the High-Level VLM is needed. Routine segments are executed by the Low-Level VLA, while complex situations such as crowded crossings trigger high-level reasoning and stop-and-wait behavior when unsafe. By combining semantic intent grounding, map-free long-horizon planning, safety-aware reasoning, and low-level action generation, Walk with Me enables practical outdoor social navigation for human-centric assistance.

</details>


### [121] [STARRY: Spatial-Temporal Action-Centric World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26848)
*Yuxuan Tian,Yurun Jin,Bin Yu,Yukun Shi,Hao Wu,Chi Harold Liu,Kai Chen,Cong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STARRY框架，通过联合去噪时空潜在表示与动作序列，并引入几何感知选择性注意力调制，在RoboTwin 2.0和真实实验中显著提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略和世界模型增强策略未能充分建模与动作相关的时空交互结构，导致对空间-时间要求高的机器人动作生成能力不足。

Method: 1) 联合去噪未来时空潜在表示与动作序列；2) 提出几何感知选择性注意力调制，将预测的深度和末端执行器几何信息转化为token对齐的权重，用于选择性动作注意力调制。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0上Clean设置平均成功率93.82%，Randomized设置93.30%；真实实验中相比π₀.₅将平均成功率从42.5%提升至70.8%。

Conclusion: 动作中心的时空世界建模对于空间-时间要求高的机器人动作生成是有效的，STARRY验证了将时空预测与动作生成对齐的优越性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation critically requires reasoning about future spatial-temporal interactions, yet existing VLA policies and world-model-enhanced policies do not fully model action-relevant spatial-temporal interaction structure. We propose STARRY, a world-model-enhanced action-generation policy that aligns spatial-temporal prediction with action generation. STARRY jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences, and introduces Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to convert predicted depth and end-effector geometry into token-aligned weights for selective action-attention modulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, STARRY achieves 93.82% / 93.30% average success under Clean and Randomized settings. Real-world experiments further improve average success from 42.5% to 70.8% over $π_{0.5}$, demonstrating the effectiveness of action-centric spatial-temporal world modeling for spatial-temporally demanding robotic action generation.

</details>


### [122] [Stochastic Entanglement of Deterministic Origami Tentacles For Universal Robotic Gripping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26897)
*Alec Boron,Bokun Zheng,Ziyang Zhou,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于折纸艺术的肌腱驱动触手夹爪，通过局部确定性变形编程与全局随机纠缠的协同，实现了对随机形状物体的鲁棒抓取，设计简洁、驱动简单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有折纸夹爪虽具有紧凑体积和机械灵活性，但在动态环境中抓取随机形状物体时，往往需要额外的驱动通道和复杂的控制系统。本文旨在通过简单的设计和驱动，实现通用物体抓取。

Method: 设计了肌腱驱动的折纸触手，通过切割Mylar薄片形成锥形结构，布置孔道和折痕以控制收缩、弯曲和扭转变形，实现单根肌腱拉动下的确定性卷曲；多个触手协同产生随机纠缠（编织、打结），从而抓取物体。结合折纸力学与Cosserat杆理论建立仿真模型。

Result: 实验验证了触手在重力环境和水中均能成功抓取物体，并展示了存储-释放部署机制，模拟了在轨抓取能力。

Conclusion: 折纸触手夹爪通过局部确定性变形与全局随机纠缠的协同，提供了一种设计简洁、驱动简单的通用物体抓取新策略。

Abstract: Origami-inspired robotic grippers have shown promising potential for object manipulation tasks due to their compact volume and mechanical flexibility. However, robust capture of objects with random shapes in dynamic working environments often comes at the cost of additional actuation channels and control complexity. Here, we introduce a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper capable of universal object gripping by exploiting a synergy between local, deterministic deformation programming and global, stochastic entanglements. Each origami tentacle is made by cutting thin Mylar sheets; It features carefully placed holes for routing an actuation tendon, origami creases for controlling the deformation, and a tapered shape. By tailoring these design features, one can prescribe the shrinking, bending, and twisting deformation, eventually creating deterministic coiling with a simple tendon pull. Then, when multiple coiling tentacles are placed in proximity, stochastic entanglement emerges, allowing the tentacles to braid, knot, and grip objects with random shapes. We derived a simulation model by integrating origami mechanics with Cosserat rods to correlate origami design, tendon deformation, and their collective gripping performance. Then, we experimentally tested how these coiling and entangling origami tentacles can grasp objects under gravity and in water. A stow-and-release deployment mechanism was also tested to simulate in-orbit grasping. Overall, the entertaining origami tentacle gripper presents a new strategy for robust object grasping with simple design and actuation.

</details>


### [123] [Bi-Level Optimization for Contact and Motion Planning in Rope-Assisted Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26910)
*Ruben Malacarne,Ioannis Tsikelis,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Michele Focchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于绳索辅助机器人爬垂直表面的双层次规划框架，外层用交叉熵方法选择落地点，内层用梯度优化计算控制输入（绳索张力、腿部力），在ALPINE平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索辅助机器人在垂直表面攀爬时，需要同时选择可行的落脚区域并优化控制输入，这是一个混合整数问题，现有方法难以高效求解。

Method: 提出双层次优化方案：外层用交叉熵方法（CEM）离散选择可行的地形落脚区域，内层用基于梯度的非线性优化计算连续控制输入（绳索张力、腿部力）和落地点位置。

Result: 在ALPINE攀爬机器人平台上，多种具有挑战性的地形配置下验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该双层次优化框架能够有效解决绳索辅助机器人攀爬中的混合整数规划问题，实现动态可行的攀爬运动。

Abstract: This paper presents a planning pipeline framework for locomotion in rope-assisted robots climbing vertical surfaces. The proposed framework is formulated as a bi-level optimization scheme that addresses a mixed-integer problem: selecting feasible terrain regions for landing while simultaneously optimizing the control inputs, namely rope tensions and leg forces, and landing location. The outer level of the optimization is solved using the Cross-Entropy Method, while the inner level relies on gradient-based nonlinear optimization to compute dynamically feasible motions. The approach is validated on a novel climbing robot platform, ALPINE, across a variety of challenging terrain configurations.

</details>
